<path>/source/standards/karpathy-guidelines.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code to avoid overcomplication, make surgical changes, surface assumptions, and define verifiable success criteria.
3: alwaysApply: true
4: ---
5: 
6: # Karpathy behavioral guidelines
7: 
8: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Merge with project-specific instructions as needed.
9: 
10: **Tradeoff:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks, use judgment.
11: 
12: ## 1. Think Before Coding
13: 
14: **Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**
15: 
16: Before implementing:
17: 
18: - State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.
19: - If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.
20: - If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.
21: - If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.
22: 
23: ## 2. Simplicity First
24: 
25: **Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.**
26: 
27: - No features beyond what was asked.
28: - No abstractions for single-use code.
29: - No "flexibility" or "configurability" that wasn't requested.
30: - No error handling for impossible scenarios.
31: - If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.
32: 
33: Ask yourself: "Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?" If yes, simplify.
34: 
35: ## 3. Surgical Changes
36: 
37: **Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.**
38: 
39: When editing existing code:
40: 
41: - Don't "improve" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.
42: - Don't refactor things that aren't broken.
43: - Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.
44: - If you notice unrelated dead code, mention it - don't delete it.
45: 
46: When your changes create orphans:
47: 
48: - Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.
49: - Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless asked.
50: 
51: The test: Every changed line should trace directly to the user's request.
52: 
53: ## 4. Goal-Driven Execution
54: 
55: **Define success criteria. Loop until verified.**
56: 
57: Transform tasks into verifiable goals:
58: 
59: - "Add validation" → "Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass"
60: - "Fix the bug" → "Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass"
61: - "Refactor X" → "Ensure tests pass before and after"
62: 
63: For multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:
64: 
65: ```
66: 1. [Step] → verify: [check]
67: 2. [Step] → verify: [check]
68: 3. [Step] → verify: [check]
69: ```
70: 
71: Strong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria ("make it work") require constant clarification.
72: 
73: ---
74: 
75: **These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes.

(End of file - total 75 lines)
</content>

Output

<path>/source/standards/ponytail.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode. Always pick the simplest solution that works.
3: globs:
4: alwaysApply: true
5: ---
6: 
7: # Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode
8: 
9: You are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. The best code is the code never written.
10: 
11: Before writing any code, stop at the first rung that holds:
12: 
13: 1. Does this need to be built at all? (YAGNI)
14: 2. Does it already exist in this codebase? Reuse the helper, util, or pattern that's already here, don't re-write it.
15: 3. Does the standard library already do this? Use it.
16: 4. Does a native platform feature cover it? Use it.
17: 5. Does an already-installed dependency solve it? Use it.
18: 6. Can this be one line? Make it one line.
19: 7. Only then: write the minimum code that works.
20: 
21: The ladder runs after you understand the problem, not instead of it: read the task and the code it touches, trace the real flow end to end, then climb.
22: 
23: Bug fix = root cause, not symptom: a report names a symptom. Grep every caller of the function you touch and fix the shared function once — one guard there is a smaller diff than one per caller, and patching only the path the ticket names leaves a sibling caller still broken.
24: 
25: Rules:
26: 
27: - No abstractions that weren't explicitly requested.
28: - No new dependency if it can be avoided.
29: - No boilerplate nobody asked for.
30: - Deletion over addition. Boring over clever. Fewest files possible.
31: - Shortest working diff wins, but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.
32: - Question complex requests: "Do you actually need X, or does Y cover it?"
33: - Pick the edge-case-correct option when two stdlib approaches are the same size, lazy means less code, not the flimsier algorithm.
34: - Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path.
35: 
36: Not lazy about: understanding the problem (read it fully and trace the real flow before picking a rung, a small diff you don't understand is just laziness dressed up as efficiency), input validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs (the platform is never the spec ideal, a clock drifts, a sensor reads off), anything explicitly requested. Lazy code without its check is unfinished: non-trivial logic leaves ONE runnable check behind, the smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks (an assert-based demo/self-check or one small test file; no frameworks, no fixtures). Trivial one-liners need no test.

(End of file - total 36 lines)
</content>

60: +func TestGraphUnderLabRuntimeRendersOnlyFromTheTopologyFile(t *testing.T) {
61: +	tests := []struct {
62: +		name    string
63: +		flags   map[string]string
64: +		wantErr bool
65: +	}{
66: +		{name: "server mode needs running containers", wantErr: true},
67: +		{name: "offline server", flags: map[string]string{"offline": "true"}},
68: +		{name: "dot", flags: map[string]string{"dot": "true"}},
69: +		{name: "mermaid", flags: map[string]string{"mermaid": "true"}},
70: +		{name: "drawio", flags: map[string]string{"drawio": "true"}},
71: +		{
72: +			name:    "explicitly not offline",
73: +			flags:   map[string]string{"offline": "false"},
74: +			wantErr: true,
75: +		},
76: +		{
77: +			name:    "node filter",
78: +			flags:   map[string]string{"mermaid": "true", "node-filter": "r1"},
79: +			wantErr: true,
80: +		},
81: +	}
82: +
83: +	for _, tt := range tests {
84: +		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
85: +			v = nil
86: +			t.Cleanup(func() { v = nil })
87: +
88: +			root := &cobra.Command{Use: "containerlab"}
89: +			graph := &cobra.Command{Use: "graph"}
90: +			root.AddCommand(graph)
91: +			for _, flag := range graphTopologyFileFlags {
92: +				graph.Flags().Bool(flag, false, "")
93: +			}
94: +			graph.Flags().StringSlice("node-filter", nil, "")
95: +			for flag, value := range tt.flags {
96: +				if err := graph.Flags().Set(flag, value); err != nil {
97: +					t.Fatal(err)
98: +				}
99: +			}
100: +
101: +			err := checkLabRuntimeCommandSupport(graph, clablabruntime.ClabernetesRuntimeName)
102: +			if (err != nil) != tt.wantErr {
103: +				t.Fatalf("checkLabRuntimeCommandSupport() error = %v, wantErr %t", err, tt.wantErr)
104: +			}
105: +
106: +			if err := checkLabRuntimeCommandSupport(graph, "docker"); err != nil {
107: +				t.Fatalf("docker graph rejected: %v", err)
108: +			}
109: +		})
110: +	}
111: +}

164: +	}

166: +	docker := labs["docker"][0]

169: +	} {

171: +			t.Fatalf("docker record carries %q: %v", field, docker)
172: +		}
173: +	}
174: +	if docker["container_id"] != "0123456789ab" {

182: +		"pod":                   "c9s-r1-abc",
183: +		"container":             "node-r1-x",
184: +		"node_uid":              "uid-1",
185: +		"load_balancer_address": "192.0.2.10",
186: +	}
187: +	for field, value := range want {
188: +		if c9s[field] != value {
189: +			t.Fatalf("c9s %s = %v, want %v (%v)", field, c9s[field], value, c9s)
190: +		}
191: +	}
192: +	ports, ok := c9s["exposed_ports"].([]any)
193: +	if !ok || len(ports) != 1 {

195: +	}
196: +	port := ports[0].(map[string]any)

198: +		port["protocol"] != "tcp" {
199: +		t.Fatalf("exposed port = %v", port)
200: +	}
201: +}

558: +func TestContainerFromLabNodeReportsWorkload(t *testing.T) {
559: +	t.Parallel()
560: +
561: +	c := &CLab{Config: &Config{Name: "lab1"}, TopoPaths: &clabtypes.TopoPaths{}}
562: +	ports := []clablabruntime.ExposedPort{{Port: 22, ExposePort: 22, Protocol: "tcp"}}
563: +
564: +	container := c.containerFromLabNode(
565: +		&clablabruntime.LabState{Name: "lab1", Namespace: "c9s-lab1"},
566: +		clablabruntime.NodeState{
567: +			Name:                "r1",
568: +			Pod:                 "lab1-r1-abc",
569: +			Container:           "node-r1-x",
570: +			NodeUID:             "uid-1",
571: +			ExposedPorts:        ports,
572: +			LoadBalancerAddress: "192.0.2.10",
573: +		},
574: +	)
575: +
576: +	if container.ID != "c9s-lab1/lab1/r1" || container.ShortID != container.ID {
577: +		t.Fatalf("ID = %q, ShortID = %q; want the unique <namespace>/<lab>/<node> id",
578: +			container.ID, container.ShortID)
579: +	}
580: +	w := container.Workload
581: +	if w == nil || w.Namespace != "c9s-lab1" || w.Pod != "lab1-r1-abc" ||
582: +		w.Container != "node-r1-x" || w.NodeUID != "uid-1" ||
583: +		w.LoadBalancerAddress != "192.0.2.10" || len(w.ExposedPorts) != 1 {
584: +		t.Fatalf("workload = %+v", w)
585: +	}
586: +

853:  ## Events
854:  
855: -The c9s runtime can stream Node, Topology, pod, and interface-stat events:

857: +interface-stat events:

861:  containerlab --runtime c9s events --initial-state

863: +containerlab --runtime c9s events --name clos --format json
864: +containerlab --runtime c9s --namespace team-a events --format json
865:  ```
866:  
867:  For c9s, events do not come from Docker events on the outer host. Containerlab
868:  watches:
869:  
870:  - c9s `Node` resources carrying `c9s.run/topologyOwner`
871:  - `Topology` resources
872:  - Pods labeled with `c9s.run/topologyOwner`
873:  
874: +What the stream covers:
875: +
876: +| Invocation | Scope |
877: +|---|---|
878: +| `events` | every namespace |
879: +| `--namespace <ns> events` or `CLAB_KUBE_NAMESPACE=<ns>` | the labs in `<ns>` |
880: +| `events -t <topo>` or `events --name <lab>` | that lab, in its namespace (`c9s-<lab>`, or the namespace override) |
881: +
882: +An identity whose RBAC only covers one namespace needs one of the scoped forms;
883: +watching every namespace requires cluster-wide `list` and `watch`.
884: +
885:  With `--initial-state`, the stream starts with synthetic events for the current
886: -c9s node state and then continues with live watches.
887: +c9s node state and the state of every interface, then continues with live
888: +watches.
889: +
890: +Container events use the node's `<namespace>/<lab>/<node>` ID as actor, the
891: +`container_id` inspect reports.
892: +
893: +### Interface events
894: +
895: +Containerlab samples the interfaces of every running node by exec into its
896: +device container. One exec per node and interval reads `/proc/net/dev` and
897: +`/sys/class/net/*/operstate` (with index, MTU, MAC address, flags and alias).
898: +All containers of a device pod share one network namespace, so the samples
899: +cover the node's pod interfaces.
900: +
901: +Interface state events are always streamed. They use the same schema as the
902: +interface events of the local runtimes, with the operational state as action:
903: +
904: +```json
905: +{
906: +  "timestamp": "2026-10-27T10:15:02.123456789Z",
907: +  "type": "interface",
908: +  "action": "down",
909: +  "actor_id": "c9s-clos/clos/srl1",
910: +  "actor_name": "clos-srl1",
911: +  "actor_full_id": "srl1-7d9c6b5f4-x2k8p",
912: +  "attributes": {
913: +    "ifname": "e1-1",
914: +    "state": "lowerlayerdown",
915: +    "index": "7",
916: +    "mtu": "9232",
917: +    "mac": "aa:c1:ab:5e:2f:01",
918: +    "lab": "clos",
919: +    "node": "srl1",
920: +    "name": "clos-srl1",
921: +    "namespace": "c9s-clos",
922: +    "pod": "srl1-7d9c6b5f4-x2k8p",
923: +    "origin": "clabernetes"
924: +  }
925: +}
926: +```
927: +
928: +- `action` is `up` when the operational state is `up`, or `unknown` on an
929: +  administratively up interface (loopback, tunnels); otherwise `down`.
930: +- `attributes.state` is the raw operational state from
931: +  `/sys/class/net/<if>/operstate`. An interface that disappears, or whose node
932: +  stops running, is reported once as `down` with state `notpresent`.
933: +- An event is emitted when an interface appears, when its action or state
934: +  changes, and, with `--initial-state`, once for every interface at the start.
935: +- Device containers without a shell report statistics only, no interface state.
936:  
937: -With `--interface-stats`, containerlab periodically reads `/proc/net/dev`
938: -inside each node's device container. All containers of a device pod share one
939: -network namespace, so the statistics cover the node's pod interfaces.
940: +With `--interface-stats`, the same exec also produces `interface stats` events
941: +with byte and packet counters and rates. They use the node ID as actor, like
942: +the state events. `--interface-stats-interval` sets the sampling interval of
943: +both (default 1s).
944:  
945:  /// note | Polling, not netlink
946: -c9s interface statistics are sampled periodically. The first sample seeds the
947: -counters, and rates start with the second sample. Short-lived changes between
948: -samples can be missed.
949: +c9s interface state and statistics are sampled periodically. The first sample
950: +seeds the counters, and rates start with the second sample. Short-lived changes
951: +between samples can be missed. Every running node gets one exec per interval,
952: +so use a longer `--interface-stats-interval` for large labs.

1109: +func newKubeClients(restConfig *rest.Config) (*kubeClients, error) {
1110: +	client, err := dynamic.NewForConfig(restConfig)
1111: +	if err != nil {
1112: +		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create Kubernetes dynamic client: %w", err)
1113: +	}
1114: +

1117: +		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create Kubernetes client: %w", err)
1118: +	}
1119: +
1120: +	return &kubeClients{dynamic: client, kube: kubeClient, restConfig: restConfig}, nil
1121: +}
1122: +
1123: +func (l *clientLoader) load() (*kubeClients, string, error) {
1124: +	l.once.Do(func() {
1125: +		restConfig, namespace, err := l.loadConfig()
1126: +		if err != nil {
1127: +			l.err = err
1128: +			return
1129: +		}
1130: +		if namespace == "" {
1131: +			namespace = defaultNamespace
1132: +		}
1133: +
1134: +		l.clients, l.err = l.newClients(restConfig)
1135: +		l.namespace = namespace
1136: +	})
1137: +
1138: +	return l.clients, l.namespace, l.err
1139: +}
1140: +
1141: +// connect returns a view of the runtime with its Kubernetes clients populated, loading the
1142: +// kubeconfig on first use. Every operation that reads or changes cluster state calls it first.
1143: +func (r *Runtime) connect() (*Runtime, error) {
1144: +	if r.connected || r.clients == nil {
1145: +		return r, nil
1146: +	}
1147: +
1148: +	clients, namespace, err := r.clients.load()
1149: +	if err != nil {
1150: +		return nil, err
1151: +	}
1152: +
1153: +	view := *r
1154: +	view.client = clients.dynamic
1155: +	view.kubeClient = clients.kube
1156: +	view.restConfig = clients.restConfig
1157: +	if view.namespace == "" {
1158: +		view.namespace = namespace
1159: +	}
1160: +	view.connected = true
1161: +
1162: +	return &view, nil
1163:  }

1922: +		sample.err = err
1923: +	case rc != 0:
1924: +		sample.err = fmt.Errorf("exit code %d: %s", rc, strings.TrimSpace(string(stderr)))
1925: +	default:
1926: +		sample.stats, sample.ifaces, sample.hasStates, sample.err = parseInterfaceSample(stdout)
1927: +	}
1928: +
1929: +	return sample
1930: +}
1931: +
1932: +// commandNotExecutable reports an exec that failed because the command does not exist in the
1933: +// container.
1934: +func commandNotExecutable(rc int, err error) bool {
1935: +	if rc == exitCommandNotExecutable || rc == exitCommandNotFound {
1936: +		return true
1937: +	}
1938: +
1939: +	return err != nil && strings.Contains(err.Error(), "executable file not found")
1940: +}
1941: +
1942: +func (p *interfacePoller) processSample(ctx context.Context, sample interfaceSample) {
1943: +	state := sample.target.state
1944: +	node := sample.target.node
1945: +	key := c9sNodeKey(state.Namespace, state.Name, node.Name)
1946: +
1947: +	tracked, known := p.nodes[key]
1948: +	if !known {
1949: +		tracked = &trackedNode{
1950: +			namespace: state.Namespace,
1951: +			lab:       state.Name,
1952: +			node:      node.Name,
1953: +			ifaces:    map[string]trackedIface{},
1954: +		}
1955: +		p.nodes[key] = tracked
1956: +	}
1957: +	tracked.pod = node.Pod
1958: +
1959: +	if p.opts.includeStats {
1960: +		for _, stat := range sample.stats {
1961: +			statsKey := c9sIfaceStatsKey(state.Namespace, state.Name, node.Name, stat.Name)
1962: +			current := c9sIfaceStatsSample{Stats: stat, Timestamp: sample.timestamp}
1963: +
1964: +			if previous, ok := p.statsSamples[statsKey]; ok {
1965: +				p.r.sendEvent(ctx, p.sink,
1966: +					c9sIfaceStatsEvent(state, node, sample.target.pod, stat, previous, current))
1967: +			}
1968: +
1969: +			p.statsSamples[statsKey] = current
1970: +		}
1971: +	}
1972: +
1973: +	if !sample.hasStates {
1974: +		return
1975: +	}
1976: +
1977: +	present := make(map[string]bool, len(sample.ifaces))
1978: +	for _, iface := range sample.ifaces {
1979: +		present[iface.Name] = true
1980: +
1981: +		action := iface.action()
1982: +		previous, seen := tracked.ifaces[iface.Name]
1983: +		tracked.ifaces[iface.Name] = trackedIface{state: iface, action: action}
1984: +
1985: +		switch {
1986: +		case seen && previous.action == action &&
1987: +			previous.state.OperState == iface.OperState:
1988: +			continue
1989: +		case !seen && p.firstRound && !p.opts.includeInitialState:
1990: +			// Interfaces that exist when the stream starts are only reported with
1991: +			// --initial-state; later changes are always reported.
1992: +			continue
1993: +		}
1994: +
1995: +		p.emitState(ctx, tracked, iface, sample.timestamp)
1996: +	}
1997: +
1998: +	for name, iface := range tracked.ifaces {
1999: +		if present[name] {
2000: +			continue
2001: +		}
2002: +
2003: +		iface.state.OperState = interfaceStateNotPresent
2004: +		p.emitState(ctx, tracked, iface.state, sample.timestamp)
2005: +		p.forgetStats(tracked, name)
2006: +		delete(tracked.ifaces, name)
2007: +	}
2008: +}
2009: +
2010: +func (p *interfacePoller) forgetStats(tracked *trackedNode, ifName string) {
2011: +	delete(p.statsSamples, c9sIfaceStatsKey(tracked.namespace, tracked.lab, tracked.node, ifName))
2012: +}
2013: +
2014: +func (p *interfacePoller) emitState(
2015: +	ctx context.Context,
2016: +	tracked *trackedNode,
2017: +	iface c9sIfaceState,
2018: +	timestamp time.Time,
2019: +) {
2020: +	p.r.sendEvent(ctx, p.sink, c9sIfaceStateEvent(tracked, iface, timestamp))
2021: +}
2022: +
2023: +// c9sIfaceStateEvent reports the operational state of an interface. The actor is the node, as
2024: +// for the container events of the lab and the interface events of the local runtimes.
2025: +func c9sIfaceStateEvent(
2026: +	tracked *trackedNode,
2027: +	iface c9sIfaceState,
2028: +	timestamp time.Time,
2029: +) clablabruntime.Event {
2030: +	actorName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-%s", tracked.lab, tracked.node)
2031: +
2032: +	attributes := map[string]string{
2033: +		"namespace": tracked.namespace,
2034: +		"lab":       tracked.lab,
2035: +		"node":      tracked.node,
2036: +		"name":      actorName,
2037: +		"ifname":    iface.Name,
2038: +		"state":     iface.OperState,
2039: +		"origin":    "clabernetes",
2040: +	}
2041: +	if tracked.pod != "" {
2042: +		attributes["pod"] = tracked.pod
2043: +	}
2044: +	if iface.Index > 0 {
2045: +		attributes["index"] = strconv.Itoa(iface.Index)
2046: +	}
2047: +	if iface.MTU > 0 {
2048: +		attributes["mtu"] = strconv.Itoa(iface.MTU)
2049: +	}
2050: +	if iface.MAC != "" {
2051: +		attributes["mac"] = iface.MAC
2052: +	}
2053: +	if iface.Alias != "" {
2054: +		attributes["alias"] = iface.Alias
2055: +	}
2056: +
2057: +	action := iface.action()
2058: +	if iface.OperState == interfaceStateNotPresent {
2059: +		action = "down"
2060: +	}
2061: +
2062: +	return clablabruntime.Event{
2063: +		Timestamp:   timestamp,
2064: +		Type:        "interface",
2065: +		Action:      action,
2066: +		ActorID:     c9sNodeKey(tracked.namespace, tracked.lab, tracked.node),
2067: +		ActorName:   actorName,
2068: +		ActorFullID: tracked.pod,
2069: +		Attributes:  attributes,
2070: +	}
2071: +}
2072: +
2073: +// parseInterfaceSample splits the output of interfaceSampleScript into the /proc/net/dev
2074: +// counters and the sysfs interface states. hasStates is false for output without the sysfs
2075: +// section, such as a plain cat of /proc/net/dev.
2076: +func parseInterfaceSample(data []byte) ([]c9sIfaceStats, []c9sIfaceState, bool, error) {
2077: +	text := string(data)
2078: +
2079: +	procPart, sysfsPart, hasStates := strings.Cut(text, interfaceSysfsMarker+"\n")
2080: +	if !hasStates {
2081: +		procPart, _, hasStates = strings.Cut(text, interfaceSysfsMarker)
2082: +	}
2083: +
2084: +	stats, err := parseProcNetDev([]byte(procPart))
2085: +	if err != nil {
2086: +		return nil, nil, false, err
2087: +	}
2088: +	if !hasStates {
2089: +		return stats, nil, false, nil
2090: +	}
2091: +
2092: +	return stats, parseSysfsInterfaces(sysfsPart), true, nil
2093: +}
2094: +
2095: +func parseSysfsInterfaces(data string) []c9sIfaceState {
2096: +	lines := strings.Split(data, "\n")
2097: +	states := make([]c9sIfaceState, 0, len(lines))
2098: +
2099: +	for _, line := range lines {
2100: +		fields := strings.SplitN(line, "\t", 7) //nolint:mnd
2101: +		if len(fields) < 5 || strings.TrimSpace(fields[0]) == "" {
2102: +			continue
2103: +		}
2104: +
2105: +		state := c9sIfaceState{
2106: +			Name:      strings.TrimSpace(fields[0]),
2107: +			MAC:       strings.TrimSpace(fields[3]),
2108: +			OperState: strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(fields[4])),
2109: +		}
2110: +		state.Index, _ = strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(fields[1]))
2111: +		state.MTU, _ = strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(fields[2]))
2112: +		if len(fields) > 5 {
2113: +			state.Flags, _ = strconv.ParseUint(
2114: +				strings.TrimPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(fields[5]), "0x"), 16, 64)
2115: +		}
2116: +		if len(fields) > 6 {
2117: +			state.Alias = strings.TrimSpace(fields[6])
2118: +		}
2119: +		if state.OperState == "" {
2120: +			state.OperState = "unknown"
2121: +		}
2122: +
2123: +		states = append(states, state)
2124: +	}
2125: +
2126: +	return states
2127:  }

2227: +// handlePersistentClaims deletes the lab's persistent node storage on a destroy with Cleanup.
2228: +// Without Cleanup it keeps the claims the lab retained and returns their number.
2229: +func (r *Runtime) handlePersistentClaims(
2230: +	ctx context.Context,
2231: +	req clablabruntime.DestroyRequest,
2232: +	namespace string,
2233: +) (int, error) {
2234: +	claims := r.kubeClient.CoreV1().PersistentVolumeClaims(namespace)
2235: +	selector := labels.Set{labelTopologyOwner: req.Name}.String()
2236: +
2237: +	list, err := claims.List(ctx, metav1.ListOptions{LabelSelector: selector})
2238: +	if err != nil {
2239: +		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list persistent claims of c9s lab %s/%s: %w",
2240: +			namespace, req.Name, err)
2241: +	}
2242: +
2243: +	if !req.Cleanup {
2244: +		retained := 0
2245: +		for idx := range list.Items {
2246: +			// Claims owned by a Node are garbage-collected with it.
2247: +			if len(list.Items[idx].OwnerReferences) == 0 {
2248: +				retained++
2249: +			}
2250: +		}
2251: +		if retained != 0 {

2253: +				"Keeping persistent node storage of C9s lab; destroy with --cleanup to delete it",
2254: +				"name", req.Name,
2255: +				"namespace", namespace,
2256: +				"claims", retained,
2257: +			)
2258: +		}
2259: +

2261: +	}
2262: +
2263: +	if len(list.Items) == 0 {
2264: +		return 0, nil
2265: +	}
2266: +
2267: +	log.Info("Deleting persistent node storage of C9s lab",
2268: +		"name", req.Name,
2269: +		"namespace", namespace,
2270: +		"claims", len(list.Items),
2271: +	)
2272: +
2273: +	for idx := range list.Items {
2274: +		err := claims.Delete(ctx, list.Items[idx].Name, metav1.DeleteOptions{})
2275: +		if err != nil && !apierrors.IsNotFound(err) {
2276: +			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to delete persistent claim %s/%s: %w",
2277: +				namespace, list.Items[idx].Name, err)
2278: +		}
2279: +	}
2280: +
2281: +	if !req.Wait {
2282: +		return 0, nil
2283: +	}

2286: +	// destroy must not adopt a claim that is still terminating.

2291: +		func(ctx context.Context) (bool, error) {

2293: +			if err != nil {
2294: +				return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to list persistent claims of c9s lab %s/%s: %w",
2295: +					namespace, req.Name, err)
2296: +			}
2297: +

2299: +		})

2303: +	}
2304: +
2305: +	return 0, nil
2306: +}

2481: +func TestNewDoesNotLoadKubeconfig(t *testing.T) {
2482: +	t.Setenv(envKubeconfig, "/nonexistent/kubeconfig")
2483: +	t.Setenv("KUBECONFIG", "/nonexistent/kubeconfig")
2484: +
2485: +	lr, err := New(clablabruntime.Config{})
2486: +	if err != nil {
2487: +		t.Fatalf("New() error = %v, want the kubeconfig to be loaded lazily", err)
2488: +	}
2489: +
2490: +	req := clablabruntime.DeployRequest{
2491: +		Name:               "lab1",
2492: +		TopologyDefinition: []byte(offlineDefinition),
2493: +	}
2494: +	if err := lr.(clablabruntime.TopologyValidator).Validate(
2495: +		context.Background(),
2496: +		req,
2497: +	); err != nil {
2498: +		t.Fatalf("Validate() error = %v, want offline validation", err)
2499: +	}
2500: +	manifests, err := lr.(clablabruntime.ManifestEmitter).Manifests(context.Background(), req)
2501: +	if err != nil || len(manifests) == 0 {
2502: +		t.Fatalf("Manifests() = %d manifests, %v; want offline manifests", len(manifests), err)
2503: +	}
2504: +
2505: +	_, err = lr.Inspect(context.Background(), clablabruntime.InspectRequest{Name: "lab1"})
2506: +	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "Kubernetes client config") {
2507: +		t.Fatalf("Inspect() error = %v, want the kubeconfig load error", err)
2508: +	}
2509: +}

2511: +func TestConnectLoadsClientsOnce(t *testing.T) {
2512: +	t.Parallel()
2513: +
2514: +	var loads atomic.Int32
2515: +	r := &Runtime{clients: &clientLoader{
2516: +		loadConfig: func() (*rest.Config, string, error) {
2517: +			loads.Add(1)
2518: +			return &rest.Config{Host: "https://cluster.example"}, "", nil
2519: +		},
2520: +		newClients: func(cfg *rest.Config) (*kubeClients, error) {
2521: +			return &kubeClients{kube: kubefake.NewSimpleClientset(), restConfig: cfg}, nil
2522: +		},
2523: +	}}
2524: +
2525: +	for range 3 {
2526: +		view, err := r.connect()
2527: +		if err != nil {
2528: +			t.Fatal(err)
2529: +		}
2530: +		if view.kubeClient == nil || view.restConfig == nil {
2531: +			t.Fatal("connected view has no clients")
2532: +		}
2533: +		if view.namespace != defaultNamespace {
2534: +			t.Fatalf("namespace = %q, want %q", view.namespace, defaultNamespace)
2535: +		}
2536: +		again, err := view.connect()
2537: +		if err != nil || again != view {
2538: +			t.Fatalf("connect() on a connected view = %p, %v; want the view itself", again, err)
2539: +		}
2540: +	}
2541: +	if loads.Load() != 1 {
2542: +		t.Fatalf("kubeconfig loaded %d times, want 1", loads.Load())
2543: +	}
2544: +	if r.kubeClient != nil {
2545: +		t.Fatal("connect() changed the shared runtime instead of returning a view")
2546: +	}
2547: +
2548: +	failing := &Runtime{clients: &clientLoader{
2549: +		loadConfig: func() (*rest.Config, string, error) {
2550: +			return nil, "", errors.New("no kubeconfig")
2551: +		},
2552: +		newClients: newKubeClients,
2553: +	}}
2554: +	if _, err := failing.connect(); err == nil {
2555: +		t.Fatal("connect() error = nil, want the load error")
2556: +	}
2557: +}

2559: +func TestListNamespacePrecedence(t *testing.T) {
2560: +	t.Parallel()

2565: +	}
2566: +	if got := r.listNamespace("", false); got != "ctx-ns" {
2567: +		t.Fatalf("no namespace = %q, want the context namespace", got)
2568: +	}
2569: +	if got := r.listNamespace("req-ns", true); got != "req-ns" {
2570: +		t.Fatalf("request namespace = %q, want req-ns", got)
2571: +	}
2572: +
2573: +	r.labNamespaceOverride = "override-ns"
2574: +	if got := r.listNamespace("", true); got != "override-ns" {
2575: +		t.Fatalf("override with all namespaces = %q, want override-ns", got)
2576: +	}
2577: +	if got := r.listNamespace("req-ns", true); got != "req-ns" {
2578: +		t.Fatalf("request namespace with override = %q, want req-ns", got)
2579: +	}
2580: +}

2582: +func TestEventScopeFor(t *testing.T) {
2583: +	t.Parallel()
2584: +
2585: +	r := &Runtime{namespace: defaultNamespace}
2586: +
2587: +	tests := []struct {
2588: +		name          string
2589: +		override      string
2590: +		req           clablabruntime.EventStreamRequest
2591: +		wantNamespace string
2592: +		wantLab       string
2593: +		wantSelector  string
2594: +	}{
2595: +		{
2596: +			name:          "all namespaces",
2597: +			req:           clablabruntime.EventStreamRequest{AllNamespaces: true},
2598: +			wantNamespace: metav1.NamespaceAll,
2599: +			wantSelector:  labelTopologyOwner,
2600: +		},
2601: +		{
2602: +			name:          "namespace override",
2603: +			override:      "team-a",
2604: +			req:           clablabruntime.EventStreamRequest{AllNamespaces: true},
2605: +			wantNamespace: "team-a",
2606: +			wantSelector:  labelTopologyOwner,
2607: +		},
2608: +		{
2609: +			name:          "lab in its canonical namespace",
2610: +			req:           clablabruntime.EventStreamRequest{Name: "lab1"},
2611: +			wantNamespace: "c9s-lab1",
2612: +			wantLab:       "lab1",
2613: +			wantSelector:  labelTopologyOwner + "=lab1",
2614: +		},
2615: +		{
2616: +			name:          "lab in the override namespace",
2617: +			override:      "team-a",
2618: +			req:           clablabruntime.EventStreamRequest{Name: "lab1"},
2619: +			wantNamespace: "team-a",
2620: +			wantLab:       "lab1",
2621: +			wantSelector:  labelTopologyOwner + "=lab1",
2622: +		},
2623: +	}
2624: +
2625: +	for _, tt := range tests {
2626: +		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
2627: +			t.Parallel()
2628: +
2629: +			view := *r
2630: +			view.labNamespaceOverride = tt.override
2631: +			scope, err := view.eventScopeFor(tt.req)
2632: +			if err != nil {
2633: +				t.Fatal(err)
2634: +			}
2635: +			if scope.namespace != tt.wantNamespace || scope.lab != tt.wantLab {
2636: +				t.Fatalf("scope = %+v, want namespace %q lab %q",
2637: +					scope, tt.wantNamespace, tt.wantLab)
2638: +			}
2639: +			if got := scope.labelSelector(); got != tt.wantSelector {
2640: +				t.Fatalf("label selector = %q, want %q", got, tt.wantSelector)
2641: +			}
2642: +			fieldSelector := scope.topologyListOptions().FieldSelector
2643: +			if (tt.wantLab != "") != (fieldSelector == "metadata.name="+tt.wantLab) {
2644: +				t.Fatalf("topology field selector = %q for lab %q", fieldSelector, tt.wantLab)
2645: +			}
2646: +		})
2647: +	}
2648: +}

2726: +	}

2730: +		}
2731: +	}

2734: +func TestEnrichStateReportsPodAndContainer(t *testing.T) {
2735: +	t.Parallel()
2736: +
2737: +	nodeResource := func(name string, spec map[string]any, status map[string]any) *unstructured.Unstructured {
2738: +		return &unstructured.Unstructured{Object: map[string]any{
2739: +			"apiVersion": c9sAPIVersion,
2740: +			"kind":       "Node",
2741: +			"metadata": map[string]any{
2742: +				"name":      name,
2743: +				"namespace": "lab-ns",
2744: +				"uid":       "uid-" + name,
2745: +				"labels":    map[string]any{labelTopologyOwner: "lab1"},
2746: +			},
2747: +			"spec":   spec,
2748: +			"status": status,
2749: +		}}
2750: +	}
2751: +	pod := func(name, node string, created time.Time, phase corev1.PodPhase) *corev1.Pod {
2752: +		return &corev1.Pod{
2753: +			ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
2754: +				Name:      name,
2755: +				Namespace: "lab-ns",
2756: +				Labels: map[string]string{
2757: +					labelApp:           clabernetesAppValue,
2758: +					labelTopologyOwner: "lab1",
2759: +					labelTopologyNode:  node,
2760: +				},
2761: +				Annotations:       map[string]string{defaultContainerAnnotation: "node-" + node},
2762: +				CreationTimestamp: metav1.NewTime(created),
2763: +			},
2764: +			Status: corev1.PodStatus{Phase: phase},
2765: +		}
2766: +	}
2767: +
2768: +	now := time.Now()
2769: +	r := newTestRuntimeWithKubeObjects(
2770: +		[]*unstructured.Unstructured{
2771: +			// observed device container
2772: +			nodeResource("r1", map[string]any{"kind": "linux"}, map[string]any{
2773: +				"readiness":        "ready",
2774: +				"directContainers": []any{map[string]any{"name": "node-r1-x", "id": "r1"}},
2775: +			}),
2776: +			// not observed yet: the pod annotation names the container
2777: +			nodeResource("r2", map[string]any{"kind": "linux"}, map[string]any{}),
2778: +			// shares the pod of r1
2779: +			nodeResource("r3", map[string]any{"kind": "linux", "network-mode": "container:r1"},
2780: +				map[string]any{
2781: +					"directContainers": []any{
2782: +						map[string]any{"name": "node-r3-y", "id": "r3"},
2783: +					},
2784: +				}),
2785: +		},
2786: +		[]k8sruntime.Object{
2787: +			pod("lab1-r1-old", "r1", now.Add(-time.Hour), corev1.PodRunning),
2788: +			pod("lab1-r1-new", "r1", now, corev1.PodRunning),
2789: +			pod("lab1-r1-pending", "r1", now.Add(time.Minute), corev1.PodPending),
2790: +			pod("lab1-r2-a", "r2", now, corev1.PodPending),
2791: +		},
2792: +	)
2793: +
2794: +	state := &clablabruntime.LabState{Name: "lab1", Namespace: "lab-ns"}
2795: +	if err := r.enrichState(context.Background(), state); err != nil {
2796: +		t.Fatal(err)
2797: +	}
2798: +
2799: +	got := map[string]clablabruntime.NodeState{}
2800: +	for _, node := range state.Nodes {
2801: +		got[node.Name] = node
2802: +	}
2803: +
2804: +	if n := got["r1"]; n.Pod != "lab1-r1-new" || n.Container != "node-r1-x" ||
2805: +		n.NodeUID != "uid-r1" {
2806: +		t.Fatalf("r1 = %+v, want the newest running pod and the observed container", n)
2807: +	}
2808: +	if n := got["r2"]; n.Pod != "lab1-r2-a" || n.Container != "node-r2" {
2809: +		t.Fatalf("r2 = %+v, want the default-container annotation", n)
2810: +	}
2811: +	if n := got["r3"]; n.Pod != "lab1-r1-new" || n.Container != "node-r3-y" {
2812: +		t.Fatalf("r3 = %+v, want the pod of r1 and its own container", n)
2813: +	}
2814: +}

2816: +func TestParseInterfaceSample(t *testing.T) {
2817: +	t.Parallel()
2818: +
2819: +	output := "Inter-|   Receive |  Transmit\n" +
2820: +		" face |bytes packets errs drop fifo frame compressed multicast|bytes packets\n" +
2821: +		"    lo: 10 1 0 0 0 0 0 0 10 1 0 0 0 0 0 0\n" +
2822: +		"  eth1: 2000 20 0 0 0 0 0 0 3000 30 0 0 0 0 0 0\n" +
2823: +		interfaceSysfsMarker + "\n" +
2824: +		"eth1\t3\t9500\taa:c1:ab:00:00:01\tup\t0x1003\tto server\n" +
2825: +		"eth2\t4\t1500\taa:c1:ab:00:00:02\tlowerlayerdown\t0x1003\t\n" +
2826: +		"lo\t1\t65536\t00:00:00:00:00:00\tunknown\t0x9\t\n" +
2827: +		"dummy0\t5\t1500\t02:00:00:00:00:01\tunknown\t0x1002\t\n"
2828: +
2829: +	stats, states, hasStates, err := parseInterfaceSample([]byte(output))
2830: +	if err != nil {
2831: +		t.Fatal(err)
2832: +	}
2833: +	if !hasStates || len(stats) != 2 || len(states) != 4 {
2834: +		t.Fatalf("stats = %+v, states = %+v, hasStates = %t", stats, states, hasStates)
2835: +	}
2836: +	if stats[1].Name != "eth1" || stats[1].RxBytes != 2000 || stats[1].TxPackets != 30 {
2837: +		t.Fatalf("eth1 stats = %+v", stats[1])
2838: +	}
2839: +
2840: +	wantActions := map[string]string{"eth1": "up", "eth2": "down", "lo": "up", "dummy0": "down"}
2841: +	for _, state := range states {
2842: +		if got := state.action(); got != wantActions[state.Name] {
2843: +			t.Fatalf(
2844: +				"%s action = %q, want %q (%+v)",
2845: +				state.Name,
2846: +				got,
2847: +				wantActions[state.Name],
2848: +				state,
2849: +			)
2850: +		}
2851: +	}
2852: +	if states[0].Index != 3 || states[0].MTU != 9500 || states[0].MAC != "aa:c1:ab:00:00:01" ||
2853: +		states[0].Alias != "to server" || states[0].Flags != 0x1003 {
2854: +		t.Fatalf("eth1 state = %+v", states[0])
2855: +	}
2856: +
2857: +	stats, states, hasStates, err = parseInterfaceSample([]byte(
2858: +		"  eth1: 2000 20 0 0 0 0 0 0 3000 30 0 0 0 0 0 0\n"))
2859: +	if err != nil || hasStates || len(states) != 0 || len(stats) != 1 {
2860: +		t.Fatalf("plain /proc/net/dev = %+v %+v %t %v", stats, states, hasStates, err)
2861: +	}
2862: +}

2864: +func TestCommandNotExecutable(t *testing.T) {
2865: +	t.Parallel()
2866: +
2867: +	if !commandNotExecutable(127, nil) || !commandNotExecutable(126, nil) {
2868: +		t.Fatal("exit codes 126/127 must mean the command cannot run")
2869: +	}
2870: +	if !commandNotExecutable(0, errors.New(`exec: "sh": executable file not found in $PATH`)) {
2871: +		t.Fatal("a missing executable must mean the command cannot run")
2872: +	}
2873: +	if commandNotExecutable(1, errors.New("connection refused")) {
2874: +		t.Fatal("other failures are not a missing command")
2875: +	}
2876: +}

2878: +func TestInterfacePollerEmitsStateChanges(t *testing.T) {
2879: +	t.Parallel()
2880: +
2881: +	newSample := func(ts time.Time, ifaces ...c9sIfaceState) interfaceSample {
2882: +		return interfaceSample{
2883: +			target: interfaceSampleTarget{
2884: +				state: &clablabruntime.LabState{Name: "lab1", Namespace: "c9s-lab1"},
2885: +				node:  clablabruntime.NodeState{Name: "r1", Pod: "lab1-r1-abc"},
2886: +				pod:   &corev1.Pod{ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{Name: "lab1-r1-abc"}},
2887: +			},
2888: +			stats: []c9sIfaceStats{
2889: +				{Name: "eth1", RxBytes: uint64(ts.Unix())},
2890: +			},
2891: +			ifaces:    ifaces,
2892: +			hasStates: true,
2893: +			timestamp: ts,
2894: +		}
2895: +	}
2896: +	up := c9sIfaceState{Name: "eth1", Index: 3, MTU: 9500, OperState: "up", Flags: 0x1003}
2897: +	down := c9sIfaceState{Name: "eth1", Index: 3, MTU: 9500, OperState: "down", Flags: 0x1002}
2898: +	eth2 := c9sIfaceState{Name: "eth2", Index: 4, OperState: "up", Flags: 0x1003}
2899: +
2900: +	for _, initialState := range []bool{false, true} {
2901: +		sink := make(chan clablabruntime.Event, 32)
2902: +		p := &interfacePoller{
2903: +			r:            &Runtime{},
2904: +			opts:         interfacePollOptions{includeInitialState: initialState},
2905: +			sink:         sink,
2906: +			statsSamples: map[string]c9sIfaceStatsSample{},
2907: +			nodes:        map[string]*trackedNode{},
2908: +			noShell:      map[string]bool{},
2909: +			firstRound:   true,
2910: +		}
2911: +		ctx := context.Background()
2912: +		start := time.Now()
2913: +
2914: +		p.processSample(ctx, newSample(start, up))
2915: +		if got := drain(sink); initialState != (len(got) == 1) {
2916: +			t.Fatalf("initial-state=%t: first round emitted %+v", initialState, got)
2917: +		}
2918: +
2919: +		p.firstRound = false
2920: +		p.processSample(ctx, newSample(start.Add(time.Second), up))
2921: +		if got := drain(sink); len(got) != 0 {
2922: +			t.Fatalf("unchanged interface emitted %+v", got)
2923: +		}
2924: +
2925: +		p.processSample(ctx, newSample(start.Add(2*time.Second), down, eth2))
2926: +		got := drain(sink)
2927: +		if len(got) != 2 {
2928: +			t.Fatalf("change emitted %+v, want eth1 down and new eth2 up", got)
2929: +		}
2930: +		byIf := map[string]clablabruntime.Event{}
2931: +		for _, ev := range got {
2932: +			byIf[ev.Attributes["ifname"]] = ev
2933: +		}

3155: +func nodeExposedPorts(nodeResource *unstructured.Unstructured) []clablabruntime.ExposedPort {
3156: +	rawPorts, _, _ := unstructured.NestedSlice(

3159: +		"exposedPorts",
3160: +		"ports",
3161: +	)

3163: +	ports := make([]clablabruntime.ExposedPort, 0, len(rawPorts))
3164: +	for _, raw := range rawPorts {
3165: +		entry, ok := raw.(map[string]any)
3166: +		if !ok {
3167: +			continue
3168: +		}
3169: +
3170: +		port := clablabruntime.ExposedPort{
3171: +			Port:       intField(entry["destinationPort"]),
3172: +			ExposePort: intField(entry["exposePort"]),
3173: +			Protocol:   strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(entry["protocol"])),
3174: +		}
3175: +		if port.Port == 0 {
3176: +			continue
3177: +		}
3178: +		if port.Protocol == "" || port.Protocol == "<nil>" {
3179: +			port.Protocol = "tcp"
3180: +		}
3181: +
3182: +		ports = append(ports, port)
3183: +	}
3184: +
3185: +	if len(ports) == 0 {
3186: +		return nil
3187: +	}
3188: +
3189: +	sort.Slice(ports, func(i, j int) bool {
3190: +		if ports[i].Port == ports[j].Port {
3191: +			return ports[i].Protocol < ports[j].Protocol
3192: +		}
3193: +
3194: +		return ports[i].Port < ports[j].Port
3195: +	})
3196: +
3197: +	return ports
3198: +}

3215: +// newestPod prefers a running pod and, among equals, the newest one: several pods can match a
3216: +// node during a rolling update.
3217: +func newestPod(current, candidate *corev1.Pod) *corev1.Pod {
3218: +	if current == nil {
3219: +		return candidate
3220: +	}
3221: +
3222: +	currentRunning := current.Status.Phase == corev1.PodRunning
3223: +	candidateRunning := candidate.Status.Phase == corev1.PodRunning
3224: +	if currentRunning != candidateRunning {
3225: +		if candidateRunning {
3226: +			return candidate
3227: +		}
3228: +
3229: +		return current
3230: +	}
3231: +
3232: +	if candidate.CreationTimestamp.After(current.CreationTimestamp.Time) {
3233: +		return candidate
3234: +	}
3235: +
3236: +	return current
3237: +}

If ctx done and errSink full, could block forever → goroutine leak. Let me check sendEventError implementation in stream or events.go (existing code).

labRuntimeUnsupportedFlags[getCommandPath(cobraCmd)] {\n\t\tif labRuntimeFlagWasSet(cobraCmd, flagName) {\n\t\t\tunsupported = append(unsupported, \"--\"+flagName)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(unsupported) {\n\t\tflag := cobraCmd.Flag(flagName)\n\t\tif flag != nil && flag.Value.String() == \"true\" &&\n\t\t\tlabRuntimeFlagWasSet(cobraCmd, flagName) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc labRuntimeFlagWasSet(cobraCmd *cobra.Command, flagName string) bool {\n\tflag := cobraCmd.Flag(flagName)\n\tif flag == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tif flag.Changed {\n\t\treturn v.IsSet(flagName)\n}\n\n// getTopoFilePath finds *.clab.y*ml file in the current working directory\n// if the file was not specified.\n// If the topology file refers to a git repository, it will be 112: 
113: func graphFn(ctx context.Context, o *Options) error {
114: 	c, err := clabcore.NewContainerLab(o.ToClabOptions()...)
115: 	if err != nil {
116: 		return err
117: 	}
118: 
119: 	// Lab runtimes do not build the topology's nodes; the graph modes they support render
120: 	// from the topology file, so the nodes are built here without a container runtime.
121: 	if err := c.LoadTopologyNodes(); err != nil {
122: 		return err
123: 	}
124: 
125: 	err = c.ResolveLinks()
126: 	if err != nil {
127: 		return err
128: 	}
129: 
130: 	if o.Graph.GenerateDotFile {
131: 		return c.GenerateDotGraph(ctx)
132: 	}
133: 
134: 	if o.Graph.GenerateMermaid {
135: 		return c.GenerateMermaidGraph(o.Graph.MermaidDirection)
136: 	}
137: 
138: 	if o.Graph.GenerateDrawIO {
139: 		return c.GenerateDrawioDiagram(o.Graph.DrawIOVersion, o.Graph.DrawIOArgs)
140: 	}
141: 
142: 	gtopo := clabcore.GraphTopo{
143: 		Nodes: make([]clabtypes.ContainerDetails, 0, len(c.Nodes)),
144: 		Links: make([]clabcore.Link, 0, len(c.Links)),
145: 	}
146: 
147: 	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
148: 	defer cancel()
149: 
150: 	var containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer
151: 	// if offline mode is not enforced, list containers matching lab name
152: 	if !o.Graph.Offline {
153: 		containers, err = c.ListContainers(ctx,
154: 			clabcore.WithListLabName(c.Config.Name))
155: 		if err != nil {
156: 			return err
157: 		}
158: 
159: 		log.Debugf("found %d containers", len(containers))
160: 	}
161: 
162: 	switch {
163: 	case len(containers) == 0:
164: 		c.BuildGraphFromTopo(&gtopo)
165: 	case len(containers) > 0:
166: 		c.BuildGraphFromDeployedLab(&gtopo, containers)
167: 	}
168: 
169: 	sort.Slice(gtopo.Nodes, func(i, j int) bool {
170: 		return gtopo.Nodes[i].Name < gtopo.Nodes[j].Name
171: 	})
172: 
173: 	for _, l := range c.Links {
174: 		eps := l.GetEndpoints()
175: 		// Skip links that are not point-to-point (e.g. dummy, host, macvlan,
176: 		// mgmt-net links expose a single endpoint). Indexing eps[1] on such
177: 		// links would panic, so they are not rendered as edges.
178: 		if len(eps) != 2 {
179: 			log.Debugf(
180: 				"skipping non point-to-point link with %d endpoint(s) in graph topology",
181: 				len(eps),
182: 			)
183: 			continue
184: 		}
185: 
186: 		ifaceDisplayNameA := eps[0].GetIfaceDisplayName()
187: 		ifaceDisplayNameB := eps[1].GetIfaceDisplayName()
188: 
189: 		gtopo.Links = append(gtopo.Links, clabcore.Link{
190: 			Source:         eps[0].GetNode().GetShortName(),
191: 			SourceEndpoint: ifaceDisplayNameA,
192: 			Target:         eps[1].GetNode().GetShortName(),
193: 			TargetEndpoint: ifaceDisplayNameB,
194: 		})
195: 	}
196: 
197: 	b, err := json.Marshal(gtopo)
198: 	if err != nil {
199: 		return err
200: 	}
201: 
202: 	log.Debugf("generating graph using data: %s", string(b))
203: 	topoD := clabcore.TopoData{
204: 		Name: c.Config.Name,
205: 		Data: template.JS(string(b)), // skipcq: GSC-G203
206: 	}
207: 
208: 	return c.ServeTopoGraph(o.Graph.Template, o.Graph.StaticDirectory, o.Graph.Server, topoD)
209: }

But the check occurs in `checkLabRuntimeCommandSupport` — when is it called? Before graphFn runs? Presumably in a PreRunE. Let me check where checkLabRuntimeCommandSupport is called. Also — a key issue: `--offline` is the default for graph in newer containerlab? Actually `o.Graph.Offline` default comes from config; historically `--offline` was a flag with default false, but there was a change where graph defaults to offline unless server? Hmm, actually in recent containerlab, `graph` serves HTML from topology + running containers. Let me check Options default for Graph.Offline. If `--offline` defaults to false, users must pass it. But note: graphFn's HTML path with offline=true builds from topo. The check demands one of the four flags explicitly set.

But here's a subtle problem: `MarkFlagsMutuallyExclusive("dot", "mermaid", "drawio")` — offline can combine.

Now check where checkLabRuntimeCommandSupport is invoked, and whether the viper binding is set for graph flags (v.IsSet(flagName)). Let me search.

64: // ParseTopology parses the lab topology.
65: func (c *CLab) parseTopology() error {
66: 	log.Info("Parsing & checking topology", "file", c.TopoPaths.TopologyFilenameBase())
67: 
68: 	if strings.Contains(c.Config.Name, gitBranchVar) ||
69: 		strings.Contains(c.Config.Name, gitHashVar) {
70: 		r := c.magicTopoNameReplacer()
71: 		oldName := c.Config.Name
72: 		c.Config.Name = r.Replace(c.Config.Name)
73: 		log.Debugf(
74: 			"Topology name contains Git variables, substituted topology name: %q -> %q",
75: 			oldName,
76: 			c.Config.Name,
77: 		)
78: 	}
79: 
80: 	err := c.TopoPaths.SetLabDirByPrefix(c.Config.Name)
81: 	if err != nil {
82: 		return err
83: 	}
84: 
85: 	if c.Config.Prefix == nil {
86: 		c.Config.Prefix = new(string)
87: 		*c.Config.Prefix = defaultPrefix
88: 	}
89: 
90: 	if err := c.validateKindSpecificConfigKeys(); err != nil {
91: 		return err
92: 	}
93: 
94: 	// initialize Nodes and Links variable
95: 	c.Nodes = make(map[string]clabnodes.Node)
96: 	c.Links = make(map[int]clablinks.Link)
97: 
98: 	// initialize the Node information from the topology map
99: 	nodeNames := make([]string, 0, len(c.Config.Topology.Nodes))
100: 	for nodeName := range c.Config.Topology.Nodes {
101: 		nodeNames = append(nodeNames, nodeName)
102: 	}
103: 
104: 	sort.Strings(nodeNames)
105: 
106: 	// collect node runtimes in a map[NodeName] -> RuntimeName
107: 	nodeRuntimes := make(map[string]string)
108: 
109: 	for nodeName, topologyNode := range c.Config.Topology.Nodes {
110: 		// this case is when runtime was overridden at the node level
111: 		if r := c.Config.Topology.GetNodeRuntime(nodeName); r != "" {
112: 			nodeRuntimes[nodeName] = r
113: 			continue
114: 		}
115: 
116: 		// this case if for non-default runtimes overriding the global default
117: 		if topologyNode != nil {
118: 			if r, ok := clabnodes.NonDefaultRuntimes[topologyNode.Kind]; ok {
119: 				nodeRuntimes[nodeName] = r
120: 				continue
121: 			}
122: 		}
123: 
124: 		// saving the global default runtime
125: 		nodeRuntimes[nodeName] = c.globalRuntimeName
126: 	}
127: 
128: 	// initialize any extra runtimes
129: 	for _, r := range nodeRuntimes {
130: 		// Nodes of a lab runtime are only described here; they are not run by a local
131: 		// container runtime.
132: 		if c.LabRuntime != nil {
133: 			break
134: 		}
135: 
136: 		// this is the case for already init'ed runtimes
137: 		if _, ok := c.Runtimes[r]; ok {
138: 			continue
139: 		}
140: 
141: 		if rInit, ok := clabruntime.ContainerRuntimes[r]; ok {
142: 			newRuntime := rInit()
143: 			defaultConfig := c.Runtimes[c.globalRuntimeName].Config()
144: 
145: 			err := newRuntime.Init(
146: 				clabruntime.WithConfig(&defaultConfig),
147: 			)
148: 			if err != nil {
149: 				return fmt.Errorf("failed to init the container runtime: %s", err)
150: 			}
151: 
152: 			c.Runtimes[r] = newRuntime
153: 		}
154: 	}
155: 
156: 	for idx, nodeName := range nodeNames {
157: 		err = c.NewNode(nodeName, nodeRuntimes[nodeName], c.Config.Topology.Nodes[nodeName], idx)
158: 		if err != nil {
159: 			return err
160: 		}
161: 	}
162: 
163: 	return c.injectTailscaleSidecars()
164: }
165: 
166: // LoadTopologyNodes builds the topology's nodes and links for a lab runtime, without a
167: // container runtime behind them. Lab runtimes skip this when they parse a topology; commands
168: // that render the topology file itself, such as graph, need the nodes. With a local runtime the
169: // nodes are already built, and it does nothing.
170: func (c *CLab) LoadTopologyNodes() error {
171: 	if c.LabRuntime == nil || !c.TopoPaths.TopologyFileIsSet() {
172: 		return nil
173: 	}
174: 
175: 	return c.parseTopology()
176: }

179: func (c *CLab) NewNode(
180: 	nodeName,
181: 	nodeRuntime string,
182: 	nodeDef *clabtypes.NodeDefinition,
183: 	idx int,
184: ) error {
185: 	nodeCfg, err := c.createNodeCfg(nodeName, nodeDef, idx)
186: 	if err != nil {
187: 		return err
188: 	}
